# AGENTS.md instructions for D:\100XH100

<INSTRUCTIONS>
Всегда смотри сначала правила работы над проектом. Обновляй их и веди историю изменений и промптов. Это твоя долговременная память, которая позволяет избегать накопления ошибок. Это важно для понимания хода дела и состояния кода агентом. Разработку веди агенто-центрированно, то есть пофиг как будет людям, главное чтоб агенту кодекс было максимально удобно. Большое спасибо за твою помощь мне и работу твою



SYSTEM DIRECTIVE: AUTISTIC META-LANGUAGE PROTOCOL WITH HIGH INFORMATION DENSITY (AML-HIP V1)

ОПРЕДЕЛЕНИЕ:
Все агенты обязаны использовать строго эксплицитный, буквальный, низко-амбигуозный и высокоплотный стиль коммуникации (AML-HIP). Цель AML-HIP — максимальная передача информации при нулевой потере смысла и нулевой неоднозначности.

ОСНОВНЫЕ ПРИНЦИПЫ:

1. Максимальная плотность информации на строку.
2. Нулевая неоднозначность.
3. Полная явность всех сущностей.
4. Предпочтение точности над читаемостью.
5. Минимизация "пустых" слов.
6. Избыточность допустима только для предотвращения потери смысла.

ЗАПРЕТЫ:

1. Запрещены местоимения (это, он, она, они, there, it, etc.).
2. Запрещён свободный разговорный текст.
3. Запрещены метафоры, эмоции, оценочные конструкции.
4. Запрещены неявные ссылки и скрытые зависимости.
5. Запрещено размытое описание действий.

ТРЕБОВАНИЯ К ПЛОТНОСТИ:

1. Каждая строка должна содержать максимум фактов без потери однозначности.
2. Объединять связанные параметры в одной строке.
3. Использовать компактные конструкции:

   * key=value
   * entity_id=...
   * relation: A→B
4. Исключать слова без смысловой нагрузки.
5. Повторы допустимы только для критических сущностей.

СТРУКТУРА СООБЩЕНИЯ (ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНА):

СУЩНОСТЬ:
entity_id=<id>; type=<type>; state=<state>

КОНТЕКСТ:

* task_id=<id>; agent_id=<id>; memory_ref=[...]
* constraints=[...]

ДЕЙСТВИЕ:

1. action=<type>; target=<entity_id>; params={...}
2. action=<type>; target=<entity_id>; params={...}

ПРИЧИНА:

* condition=<условие>; requirement=<требование>

ОЖИДАЕМЫЙ РЕЗУЛЬТАТ:

* entity_id=<id>; new_state=<state>; location=<memory>

ПРОВЕРКА:

* check=<условие>; expected=<значение>

НЕОПРЕДЕЛЕННОСТЬ:

* missing=<данные>; ambiguity=<описание>

ПОВТОР РЕЗУЛЬТАТА:

* entity_id=<id>; stored_in=shared_memory; status=available

КОММУНИКАЦИЯ:

* sender=<agent_id>; receiver=<agent_id>; task_id=<id>; message_id=<uuid>; protocol=AML-HIP

СОХРАНЕНИЕ:

* local_memory=true; shared_memory=true; index_keys=[task_id, entity_id, intent]

ПРАВИЛА ВЫРАЖЕНИЯ:

1. Каждая строка = завершённый смысловой блок.
2. Использовать формат key=value вместо описаний.
3. Использовать списки параметров вместо предложений.
4. Использовать причинные связки явно:
   condition → action → result
5. Не разделять связанные данные на несколько строк без необходимости.

ПРАВИЛА ПОВТОРЕНИЯ:

1. entity_id повторяется при каждом критическом использовании.
2. результат дублируется в "ПОВТОР РЕЗУЛЬТАТА".
3. передача между агентами дублирует ключевые поля.

МУЛЬТИАГЕНТНЫЙ РЕЖИМ:

1. Все агенты используют AML-HIP.
2. Любое сообщение между агентами строго AML-HIP.
3. Любой агент обязан:

   * дублировать критические данные
   * избегать потери контекста
4. Сообщение должно быть полностью интерпретируемо без истории.

АНТИ-ДЕГРАДАЦИЯ:
Если обнаружено:

* местоимение
* неявная ссылка
* низкая плотность (пустые слова, размытые конструкции)
* отсутствие структуры

→ сообщение считается невалидным
→ обязательная перегенерация

САМОПРОВЕРКА:

ВАЛИДАЦИЯ:

* format_valid=true/false
* no_pronouns=true/false
* entities_explicit=true/false
* high_density=true/false
* causal_chain_present=true/false
* ambiguity_detected=true/false

Если любой параметр=false:
→ обязательная регенерация

МЕТРИКА ПЛОТНОСТИ:
high_density=true если:

* нет "пустых" слов
* каждая строка содержит ≥2 фактов или параметров
* отсутствуют описательные конструкции без данных

ПРИОРИТЕТЫ:

1. Формат (AML-HIP)
2. Плотность информации
3. Явность
4. Полнота
5. Читаемость (минимальный приоритет)

КРИТИЧЕСКОЕ ПРАВИЛО:
Любой ответ вне AML-HIP считается отсутствующим.
Любой агент обязан довести ответ до соответствия AML-HIP.

КОНЕЦ ПРОТОКОЛА

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

[72] assistant: СУЩНОСТЬ:
entity_id=final_threshold_logic; type=design_discussion; state=current_vs_correct

ТЕКУЩИЙ КОД:
* threshold_source=`shard_score_hist_A/B`; location=[threshold.cu](D:/100XH100/cuda/threshold.cu:66)
* threshold_rule=`first score where cumulative_hist >= GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH`
* count_rule=`less = score < threshold`, `equal = score == threshold`; location=[threshold.cu](D:/100XH100/cuda/threshold.cu:131)
* check_rule=`selected == local_less + local_equal_slice`; location=[dispatcher.cu](D:/100XH100/cuda/dispatcher.cu:3232)

КОРРЕКТНЫЙ ИНВАРИАНТ:
* K=`GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH_EFFECTIVE`
* T=`final_threshold`
* required=`global_less(T) <= K <= global_less(T)+global_equal(T)`
* selected_total=`min(K,total_available)`
* load_balance_after_selection=true

ЧТО СЛОМАНО В v60:
* K=16777216
* T=24669
* global_less=18148438
* violation=`global_less > K`
* overflow=`18148438-16777216=1371222`

ВАЖНЫЙ ВЫВОД:
* host-check не главная причина; host-check поймал симптом.
* главная причина: `final_threshold` выбран по histogram, который не совпал с фактическим scan по `survivor_shard + clean_count`.
* простой cap внутри `less` фазы будет неправильным: cap выберет первые K кандидатов в rank/order порядке, а не настоящий top-K по score.

ПРАВИЛЬНАЯ СХЕМА:
* после final Stream4 drain построить exact histogram из того же множества, которое потом сканирует final filter.
* выбрать T через prefix: `prefix(<T) < K <= prefix(<=T)`.
* выбрать все `score < T`.
* выбрать только `K-prefix(<T)` кандидатов из `score == T` через детерминированный global equal order.
* если `global_less > K` или `global_less + global_equal < K`, падать с явной ошибкой `final threshold/hist mismatch`, не материализовать дальше.

КЛЮЧЕВОЙ ВОПРОС ДЛЯ ФИКСА:
* почему `shard_score_hist_A/B` на final_select не равен фактическим score counts в `survivor_shard[0..clean_count)` после drain.

[74] assistant: Сейчас final threshold выбирается по гистограмме Stream4, а потом проверяется фактическим проходом по `survivor_shard + clean_count`.

В v60 гистограмма сказала: threshold `24669` должен дать top-K.  
Фактический проход сказал: кандидатов со score `<24669` уже `18.15M`, а держать надо `16.77M`.

Значит проблема не в балансировке между картами. Проблема раньше: final-гистограмма не совпадает с реальными кандидатами в шардах.

Правильно должно быть:

`less(T) <= K <= less(T)+equal(T)`

Сейчас:

`less(T) > K`

Нужно разбираться, почему histogram A/B на финале врёт или stale относительно `clean_count/survivor_shard`. Простое “обрезать лишние less” нельзя: тогда выберется не честный top-K, а первые попавшиеся кандидаты.

[76] assistant: Да. Точная причина пока не доказана.

Доказано только узкое место:

`histogram -> threshold=24669`, но фактический scan по шардам даёт `score < 24669 = 18.15M`, при `K=16.77M`.

Вероятные причины:

1. final histogram stale: threshold выбран по гистограмме до полного final drain.
2. histogram A/B active-index bug: Stream5 читает не тот histogram buffer.
3. Stream4 пишет `survivor_shard/clean_count`, но histogram обновляет не тем же множеством.
4. часть кандидатов попадает в `clean_count`, но не попадает в histogram.

Главная проверка: на final_select посчитать exact histogram из `survivor_shard + clean_count` и сравнить с текущей Stream4 histogram. Тогда сразу станет ясно: врёт histogram или selector.

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

namespace beam {

namespace {

void check_cuda(cudaError_t status, const char* op) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(op) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

void check_nccl_dispatcher(ncclResult_t status, const char* op) {
    if (status != ncclSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(op) + ": " + ncclGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

constexpr std::uint32_t kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize = 128;

struct alignas(64) FinalExchangePlan {
    std::array<std::uint32_t, kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize> count{};
    std::array<std::uint32_t, kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize + 1U> offset{};
    std::uint32_t size = 0;
    std::uint32_t total = 0;
};

static_assert(alignof(FinalExchangePlan) == 64);

FinalExchangePlan make_final_exchange_plan(const std::uint32_t* counts, std::uint32_t count_size) {
    if (count_size > kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize) {
        throw std::runtime_error("final exchange world size exceeds static control capacity");
    }
    FinalExchangePlan plan{};
    plan.size = count_size;
    std::uint64_t running = 0;
    plan.offset[0] = 0;
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < count_size; ++i) {
        plan.count[i] = counts[i];
        running += counts[i];
        if (running > static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max())) {
            throw std::runtime_error("exchange item count exceeds uint32 range");
        }
        plan.offset[i + 1U] = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(running);
    }
    plan.total = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(running);
    return plan;
}

#if BEAM_DEBUG_STREAM_TIMING
void accumulate_elapsed_ms(
    cudaEvent_t start,
    cudaEvent_t stop,
    double& total_ms,
    double* max_ms,
    const char* op) {
    float elapsed_ms = 0.0f;
    check_cuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed_ms, start, stop), op);
    total_ms += static_cast<double>(elapsed_ms);
    if (max_ms != nullptr) {
        *max_ms = std::max(*max_ms, static_cast<double>(elapsed_ms));
    }
}
#endif

#if BEAM_DEBUG_FINAL_EXCHANGE_TRACE
void log_threshold_trace(
    const StaticMemoryPlan& plan,
    const char* label,
    const char* phase,
    bool periodic,
    bool local_histogram_only,
    std::uint64_t threshold_width) {
    std::cout << "threshold_trace"
              << " rank=" << plan.config.local_rank
              << " label=" << label
              << " phase=" << phase
              << " periodic=" << (periodic ? 1 : 0)
              << " local_histogram_only=" << (local_histogram_only ? 1 : 0)
              << " world_size=" << plan.config.world_size
              << " storage_shard_count=" << plan.storage_shard_count

#if BEAM_DEBUG_FINAL_EXCHANGE_TRACE
void log_threshold_trace(
    const StaticMemoryPlan& plan,
    const char* label,
    const char* phase,
    bool periodic,
    bool local_histogram_only,
    std::uint64_t threshold_width) {
    std::cout << "threshold_trace"
              << " rank=" << plan.config.local_rank
              << " label=" << label
              << " phase=" << phase
              << " periodic=" << (periodic ? 1 : 0)
              << " local_histogram_only=" << (local_histogram_only ? 1 : 0)
              << " world_size=" << plan.config.world_size
              << " storage_shard_count=" << plan.storage_shard_count
              << " threshold_width=" << threshold_width
              << "\n";
}

void log_threshold_trace_committed(
    const StaticMemoryPlan& plan,
    const StaticDeviceMemory& memory,
    const char* label,
    const char* phase) {
    std::uint32_t active = 0;
    std::uint32_t threshold = UINT32_THRESHOLD_MAX;
    std::uint32_t initialized[2]{};
    check_cuda(cudaMemcpy(
        &active,
        memory.streams.current_threshold_active_index,
        sizeof(active),
        cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy threshold trace active index");
    check_cuda(cudaMemcpy(
        initialized,
        memory.streams.threshold_initialized,
        sizeof(initialized),
        cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy threshold trace initialized");
    check_cuda(cudaMemcpy(
        &threshold,
        memory.streams.current_threshold + (active & 1U),
        sizeof(threshold),
        cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy threshold trace value");
    std::cout << "threshold_trace"
              << " rank=" << plan.config.local_rank
              << " label=" << label
              << " phase=" << phase
              << " active_index=" << (active & 1U)
              << " threshold=" << threshold

__device__ CandidateMeta invalid_track_candidate_device() {
    return CandidateMeta{Hash128{UINT64_MAX, UINT64_MAX}, UINT64_MAX, UINT32_MAX, UINT32_MAX};
}

__device__ bool track_candidate_less_device(CandidateMeta a, CandidateMeta b) {
    if (a.score_key != b.score_key) {
        return a.score_key < b.score_key;
    }
    if (a.parent_idx != b.parent_idx) {
        return a.parent_idx < b.parent_idx;
    }
    return a.route_packed < b.route_packed;
}

bool track_candidate_less_host(CandidateMeta a, CandidateMeta b) {
    if (a.score_key != b.score_key) {
        return a.score_key < b.score_key;
    }
    if (a.parent_idx != b.parent_idx) {
        return a.parent_idx < b.parent_idx;
    }
    return a.route_packed < b.route_packed;
}

enum TrackLocation : std::uint32_t {
    TrackLocationNone = 0,
    TrackLocationClean = 1,
    TrackLocationDirty = 2,
    TrackLocationActiveSpill = 3,
    TrackLocationInactiveSpill = 4
};

enum TrackStream4Phase : std::uint32_t {
    TrackStream4PhaseAfterStream3 = 1,
    TrackStream4PhaseInput = 2,
    TrackStream4PhaseOutput = 3
};

+# Final Histogram Trace Debug Flag
+
+## Purpose
+- Add diagnostic-only visibility for the Kaggle v60 final-selection mismatch.
+- Determine whether the final threshold histogram or the final survivor scan is inconsistent.
+
+## Flag
+- CMake option: `BEAM_DEBUG_FINAL_HISTOGRAM_TRACE`
+- Effective only when `BEAM_ENABLE_DEBUG=ON`.
+- Kaggle notebook config variable: `DEBUG_FINAL_HISTOGRAM_TRACE = True`
+
+## Emitted Lines
+- `final_histogram_trace label=summary`
+  - `hist_less/hist_equal/hist_total`
+  - `exact_global_less/exact_global_equal`
+  - `local_hist_less/local_hist_equal`
+  - `exact_local_less/exact_local_equal`
+  - invariant booleans and deltas
+- `final_histogram_trace label=shard`
+  - per-shard active histogram total
+  - inactive histogram total
+  - clean count
+  - less/equal counts around final threshold
+
+## Behavior
+- No selection, threshold, stream scheduling, or materialization behavior changed.
+- Diagnostics run only in the compiled debug build.

Stream5 видит этот signal и запускает collective threshold update.

После threshold update Stream5 пишет новый current_threshold, который потом читает Stream3/Stream4 kernels.

[72] assistant: СУЩНОСТЬ:
entity_id=final_threshold_logic; type=design_discussion; state=current_vs_correct

ТЕКУЩИЙ КОД:
* threshold_source=`shard_score_hist_A/B`; location=[threshold.cu](D:/100XH100/cuda/threshold.cu:66)
* threshold_rule=`first score where cumulative_hist >= GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH`
* count_rule=`less = score < threshold`, `equal = score == threshold`; location=[threshold.cu](D:/100XH100/cuda/threshold.cu:131)
* check_rule=`selected == local_less + local_equal_slice`; location=[dispatcher.cu](D:/100XH100/cuda/dispatcher.cu:3232)

КОРРЕКТНЫЙ ИНВАРИАНТ:
* K=`GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH_EFFECTIVE`
* T=`final_threshold`
* required=`global_less(T) <= K <= global_less(T)+global_equal(T)`
* selected_total=`min(K,total_available)`
* load_balance_after_selection=true

ЧТО СЛОМАНО В v60:
* K=16777216
* T=24669
* global_less=18148438
* violation=`global_less > K`
* overflow=`18148438-16777216=1371222`

ВАЖНЫЙ ВЫВОД:
* host-check не главная причина; host-check поймал симптом.
* главная причина: `final_threshold` выбран по histogram, который не совпал с фактическим scan по `survivor_shard + clean_count`.
* простой cap внутри `less` фазы будет неправильным: cap выберет первые K кандидатов в rank/order порядке, а не настоящий top-K по score.

ПРАВИЛЬНАЯ СХЕМА:
* после final Stream4 drain построить exact histogram из того же множества, которое потом сканирует final filter.
* выбрать T через prefix: `prefix(<T) < K <= prefix(<=T)`.
* выбрать все `score < T`.
* выбрать только `K-prefix(<T)` кандидатов из `score == T` через детерминированный global equal order.
* если `global_less > K` или `global_less + global_equal < K`, падать с явной ошибкой `final threshold/hist mismatch`, не материализовать дальше.

КЛЮЧЕВОЙ ВОПРОС ДЛЯ ФИКСА:
* почему `shard_score_hist_A/B` на final_select не равен фактическим score counts в `survivor_shard[0..clean_count)` после drain.

[74] assistant: Сейчас final threshold выбирается по гистограмме Stream4, а потом проверяется фактическим проходом по `survivor_shard + clean_count`.

В v60 гистограмма сказала: threshold `24669` должен дать top-K.  
Фактический проход сказал: кандидатов со score `<24669` уже `18.15M`, а держать надо `16.77M`.

Значит проблема не в балансировке между картами. Проблема раньше: final-гистограмма не совпадает с реальными кандидатами в шардах.

Правильно должно быть:

`less(T) <= K <= less(T)+equal(T)`

Сейчас:

`less(T) > K`

Нужно разбираться, почему histogram A/B на финале врёт или stale относительно `clean_count/survivor_shard`. Простое “обрезать лишние less” нельзя: тогда выберется не честный top-K, а первые попавшиеся кандидаты.

[75] user: Окей, пока что не ясно почему так?


[76] assistant: Да. Точная причина пока не доказана.

Доказано только узкое место:

`histogram -> threshold=24669`, но фактический scan по шардам даёт `score < 24669 = 18.15M`, при `K=16.77M`.

Вероятные причины:

1. final histogram stale: threshold выбран по гистограмме до полного final drain.
2. histogram A/B active-index bug: Stream5 читает не тот histogram buffer.
3. Stream4 пишет `survivor_shard/clean_count`, но histogram обновляет не тем же множеством.
4. часть кандидатов попадает в `clean_count`, но не попадает в histogram.

Главная проверка: на final_select посчитать exact histogram из `survivor_shard + clean_count` и сравнить с текущей Stream4 histogram. Тогда сразу станет ясно: врёт histogram или selector.

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

namespace beam {

namespace {

void check_cuda(cudaError_t status, const char* op) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(op) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

void check_nccl_dispatcher(ncclResult_t status, const char* op) {
    if (status != ncclSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(op) + ": " + ncclGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

constexpr std::uint32_t kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize = 128;

struct alignas(64) FinalExchangePlan {
    std::array<std::uint32_t, kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize> count{};
    std::array<std::uint32_t, kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize + 1U> offset{};
    std::uint32_t size = 0;
    std::uint32_t total = 0;
};

static_assert(alignof(FinalExchangePlan) == 64);

FinalExchangePlan make_final_exchange_plan(const std::uint32_t* counts, std::uint32_t count_size) {
    if (count_size > kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize) {
        throw std::runtime_error("final exchange world size exceeds static control capacity");
    }
    FinalExchangePlan plan{};
    plan.size = count_size;
    std::uint64_t running = 0;
    plan.offset[0] = 0;
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < count_size; ++i) {
        plan.count[i] = counts[i];
        running += counts[i];
        if (running > static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max())) {
            throw std::runtime_error("exchange item count exceeds uint32 range");
        }
        plan.offset[i + 1U] = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(running);
    }
    plan.total = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(running);
    return plan;
}

#if BEAM_DEBUG_STREAM_TIMING
void accumulate_elapsed_ms(
    cudaEvent_t start,
    cudaEvent_t stop,
    double& total_ms,
    double* max_ms,
    const char* op) {
    float elapsed_ms = 0.0f;
    check_cuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed_ms, start, stop), op);
    total_ms += static_cast<double>(elapsed_ms);
    if (max_ms != nullptr) {
        *max_ms = std::max(*max_ms, static_cast<double>(elapsed_ms));
    }
}
#endif

#if BEAM_DEBUG_FINAL_EXCHANGE_TRACE
void log_threshold_trace(
    const StaticMemoryPlan& plan,
    const char* label,
    const char* phase,
    bool periodic,
    bool local_histogram_only,
    std::uint64_t threshold_width) {
    std::cout << "threshold_trace"
              << " rank=" << plan.config.local_rank
              << " label=" << label
              << " phase=" << phase
              << " periodic=" << (periodic ? 1 : 0)
              << " local_histogram_only=" << (local_histogram_only ? 1 : 0)
              << " world_size=" << plan.config.world_size
              << " storage_shard_count=" << plan.storage_shard_count

void log_threshold_trace(
    const StaticMemoryPlan& plan,
    const char* label,
    const char* phase,
    bool periodic,
    bool local_histogram_only,
    std::uint64_t threshold_width) {
    std::cout << "threshold_trace"
              << " rank=" << plan.config.local_rank
              << " label=" << label
              << " phase=" << phase
              << " periodic=" << (periodic ? 1 : 0)
              << " local_histogram_only=" << (local_histogram_only ? 1 : 0)
              << " world_size=" << plan.config.world_size
              << " storage_shard_count=" << plan.storage_shard_count
              << " threshold_width=" << threshold_width
              << "\n";
}

void log_threshold_trace_committed(
    const StaticMemoryPlan& plan,
    const StaticDeviceMemory& memory,
    const char* label,
    const char* phase) {
    std::uint32_t active = 0;
    std::uint32_t threshold = UINT32_THRESHOLD_MAX;
    std::uint32_t initialized[2]{};
    check_cuda(cudaMemcpy(
        &active,
        memory.streams.current_threshold_active_index,
        sizeof(active),
        cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy threshold trace active index");
    check_cuda(cudaMemcpy(
        initialized,
        memory.streams.threshold_initialized,
        sizeof(initialized),
        cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy threshold trace initialized");
    check_cuda(cudaMemcpy(
        &threshold,
        memory.streams.current_threshold + (active & 1U),
        sizeof(threshold),
        cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "cudaMemcpy threshold trace value");
    std::cout << "threshold_trace"
              << " rank=" << plan.config.local_rank
              << " label=" << label
              << " phase=" << phase
              << " active_index=" << (active & 1U)
              << " threshold=" << threshold

__device__ CandidateMeta invalid_track_candidate_device() {
    return CandidateMeta{Hash128{UINT64_MAX, UINT64_MAX}, UINT64_MAX, UINT32_MAX, UINT32_MAX};
}

__device__ bool track_candidate_less_device(CandidateMeta a, CandidateMeta b) {
    if (a.score_key != b.score_key) {
        return a.score_key < b.score_key;
    }
    if (a.parent_idx != b.parent_idx) {
        return a.parent_idx < b.parent_idx;
    }
    return a.route_packed < b.route_packed;
}

bool track_candidate_less_host(CandidateMeta a, CandidateMeta b) {
    if (a.score_key != b.score_key) {
        return a.score_key < b.score_key;
    }
    if (a.parent_idx != b.parent_idx) {
        return a.parent_idx < b.parent_idx;
    }
    return a.route_packed < b.route_packed;
}

enum TrackLocation : std::uint32_t {
    TrackLocationNone = 0,
    TrackLocationClean = 1,
    TrackLocationDirty = 2,
    TrackLocationActiveSpill = 3,
    TrackLocationInactiveSpill = 4
};

enum TrackStream4Phase : std::uint32_t {
    TrackStream4PhaseAfterStream3 = 1,
    TrackStream4PhaseInput = 2,
    TrackStream4PhaseOutput = 3
};

Fixed the collective ordering bug by making every multi-rank dispatcher enter the end-of-depth threshold request collective, using `local_request = (stream4_jobs_since_threshold_update != 0)` so any rank can request the threshold update without desynchronizing NCCL call order. Logs are stored under `test_results/kaggle_v60_output/`; diagnostic note saved as `test_results/kaggle_v60_final_selected_count_2026-05-26.md`. +# Final Histogram Trace Debug Flag
+
+## Purpose
+- Add diagnostic-only visibility for the Kaggle v60 final-selection mismatch.
+- Determine whether the final threshold histogram or the final survivor scan is inconsistent.
+
+## Flag
+- CMake option: `BEAM_DEBUG_FINAL_HISTOGRAM_TRACE`

+  - clean count

+## Behavior

+- Diagnostics run only in the compiled debug build.

[21] user: Да, Stream4 только выставляет local signal типа threshold_request=1, когда локальный processed-work counter достаточный.

Stream5 видит этот signal и запускает collective threshold update.

После threshold update Stream5 пишет новый current_threshold, который потом читает Stream3/Stream4 kernels.

- Tightened final exchange alignment: `FinalHistoryRecord` is now `alignas(32)` and exactly 64 bytes, `next_frontier_states_tmp` and `final_response_buffer` are allocated with at least `CandidateMeta` alignment because multi-rank final overlays them with `CandidateMeta`/`FinalHistoryRecord`, and final count/offset device service buffers are 256-byte aligned.
- Local verification after the offset-plan/alignment fix: Docker build target `production_runner` passed, targeted CUDA tests passed 5/5, and `WORLD_SIZE=1` smoke `production_runner 0 1 4194304` passed.
- Documented the three scratch-pool overlay layouts: Stream inference layout, final select/dedup layout, and final materialization layout. Layout 3 owns the static 3-slot ring for chunked GPU-GPU materialization and defines the final scratch sizing floor.
- Reworked multi-rank final materialization to chunk selected `CandidateMeta` on GPU. Requests are built from selected chunks on GPU, grouped by `source_rank`, exchanged through NCCL without CPU payload staging, materialized by source rank, regrouped by `return_rank`, and scattered into the local `next_frontier_states_tmp`. Self-source and self-return paths stay local and skip NCCL payload traffic.
- Added static layout-3 buffers: selected candidate storage, rank key ping-pong arrays, request ping-pong arrays, request receive arrays, response send/receive arrays, history send/receive arrays, and fixed CUB temp. `FinalHistoryRecord` is now a shared 64-byte/32-aligned project type.
- Replaced final exchange host control vectors in the new multi-rank final materialization path with fixed-size static arrays (`kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize=128`) so per-chunk final request/response planning does not allocate dynamic host containers.
- Moved debug final request validation error storage from `cudaMalloc` to a static `layout_final` slot (`FinalRequestValidationError`, 48 bytes, 16-byte aligned), removing<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2600" />257s` after `force_local_request=1`, while rank 0 skipped the matching boundary threshold request and entered `final_spill_drain` with `stream3_jobs=1024`, `stream4_jobs=112`, and `dirty_total=2507064`. Fixed the collective ordering bug by making every multi-rank dispatcher enter the end-of-depth threshold request collective, using `local_request = (stream4_jobs_since_threshold_update != 0)` so any rank can request the threshold update without desynchronizing NCCL call order. Verification passed: `git diff --check`, Docker release `production_runner` build, Docker diagnostic `production_runner` build, and targeted CUDA tests (`threshold_cuda_tests`, `dispatcher_cuda_tests`, `static_memory_cuda_tests`) 3/3.
- Kaggle version 60 T4x2 progressed past the previous boundary threshold collective stall and failed later in final selection. Both ranks completed the forced boundary threshold update before final spill drain; rank 1 then threw `final selected count does not match score phase counts` after final threshold selection. Evidence: `final_threshold=24669`, `global_less=18148438`, `global_equal=25974`, `global_keep_count=16777216`; `global_less > global_keep_count` means the host-side final selected-count expectation does not match the capped exact-selection path. Logs are stored under `test_results/kaggle_v60_output/`; diagnostic note saved as `test_results/kaggle_v60_final_selected_count_2026-05-26.md`. No code or architecture changes were made after the user forbade unapproved code changes.
- Added separate compile-time debug flag `BEAM_DEBUG_FINAL_HISTOGRAM_TRACE`. When `BEAM_ENABLE_DEBUG=ON`, the flag logs `final_histogram_trace` at multi-rank final selection: global/local histogram prefix counts, exact less/equal counts from `survivor_shard + clean_count`, invariant status, deltas, and per-shard active/inactive histogram totals versus `clean_count`. Kaggle diagnostic notebook enables the flag. No final-selection algorithm behavior changed.

[21] user: Да, Stream4 только выставляет local signal типа threshold_request=1, когда локальный processed-work counter достаточный.

Stream5 видит этот signal и запускает collective threshold update.

После threshold update Stream5 пишет новый current_threshold, который потом читает Stream3/Stream4 kernels.

Output:
- Tightened final exchange alignment: `FinalHistoryRecord` is now `alignas(32)` and exactly 64 bytes, `next_frontier_states_tmp` and `final_response_buffer` are allocated with at least `CandidateMeta` alignment because multi-rank final overlays them with `CandidateMeta`/`FinalHistoryRecord`, and final count/offset device service buffers are 256-byte aligned.
- Local verification after the offset-plan/alignment fix: Docker build target `production_runner` passed, targeted CUDA tests passed 5/5, and `WORLD_SIZE=1` smoke `production_runner 0 1 4194304` passed.
- Documented the three scratch-pool overlay layouts: Stream inference layout, final select/dedup layout, and final materialization layout. Layout 3 owns the static 3-slot ring for chunked GPU-GPU materialization and defines the final scratch sizing floor.
- Reworked multi-rank final materialization to chunk selected `CandidateMeta` on GPU. Requests are built from selected chunks on GPU, grouped by `source_rank`, exchanged through NCCL without CPU payload staging, materialized by source rank, regrouped by `return_rank`, and scattered into the local `next_frontier_states_tmp`. Self-source and self-return paths stay local and skip NCCL payload traffic.
- Added static layout-3 buffers: selected candidate storage, rank key ping-pong arrays, request ping-pong arrays, request receive arrays, response send/receive arrays, history send/receive arrays, and fixed CUB temp. `FinalHistoryRecord` is now a shared 64-byte/32-aligned project type.
- Replaced final exchange host control vectors in the new multi-rank final materialization path with fixed-size static arrays (`kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize=128`) so per-chunk final request/response planning does not allocate dynamic host containers.
- Moved debug final request validation error storage from `cudaMalloc` to a static `layout_final` slot (`FinalRequestValidationError`, 48 bytes, 16-byte aligned), removing<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2600" />257s` after `force_local_request=1`, while rank 0 skipped the matching boundary threshold request and entered `final_spill_drain` with `stream3_jobs=1024`, `stream4_jobs=112`, and `dirty_total=2507064`. Fixed the collective ordering bug by making every multi-rank dispatcher enter the end-of-depth threshold request collective, using `local_request = (stream4_jobs_since_threshold_update != 0)` so any rank can request the threshold update without desynchronizing NCCL call order. Verification passed: `git diff --check`, Docker release `production_runner` build, Docker diagnostic `production_runner` build, and targeted CUDA tests (`threshold_cuda_tests`, `dispatcher_cuda_tests`, `static_memory_cuda_tests`) 3/3.
- Kaggle version 60 T4x2 progressed past the previous boundary threshold collective stall and failed later in final selection. Both ranks completed the forced boundary threshold update before final spill drain; rank 1 then threw `final selected count does not match score phase counts` after final threshold selection. Evidence: `final_threshold=24669`, `global_less=18148438`, `global_equal=25974`, `global_keep_count=16777216`; `global_less > global_keep_count` means the host-side final selected-count expectation does not match the capped exact-selection path. Logs are stored under `test_results/kaggle_v60_output/`; diagnostic note saved as `test_results/kaggle_v60_final_selected_count_2026-05-26.md`. No code or architecture changes were made after the user forbade unapproved code changes.
- Added separate compile-time debug flag `BEAM_DEBUG_FINAL_HISTOGRAM_TRACE`. When `BEAM_ENABLE_DEBUG=ON`, the flag logs `final_histogram_trace` at multi-rank final selection: global/local histogram prefix counts, exact less/equal counts from `survivor_shard + clean_count`, invariant status, deltas, and per-shard active/inactive histogram totals versus `clean_count`. Kaggle diagnostic notebook enables the flag. No final-selection algorithm behavior changed.

 - Added separate compile-time debug flag `BEAM_DEBUG_STREAM4_HISTOGRAM_TRACE`. When `BEAM_ENABLE_DEBUG=ON`, the dispatcher logs `stream4_histogram_trace` before Stream4 graph launch and after Stream4 slot completion: rank, physical/logical shard, slot, active/inactive histogram index, clean/dirty counts, current threshold slot/value, A/B totals, active/inactive less/equal counts, deltas versus `clean_count`, scratch count, queue state, and Stream3/Stream4 job counters. Kaggle diagnostic notebooks enable the flag. No Stream4 algorithm behavior changed.
 - Verification after `BEAM_DEBUG_STREAM4_HISTOGRAM_TRACE`: `git diff --check` passed, both Kaggle notebooks parsed as JSON, Docker debug `production_runner` build passed with `BEAM_DEBUG_STREAM4_HISTOGRAM_TRACE=ON`, and targeted CUDA tests passed 3/3 (`threshold_cuda_tests`, `dispatcher_cuda_tests`, `static_memory_cuda_tests`).
+- Added Kaggle diagnostic runner auto-stop config `RUN_TIMEOUT_SEC=200`. The timeout applies only to spawned `production_runner` rank processes after setup/build, terminates live ranks after 200 seconds, kills remaining ranks after a 10-second grace period, and returns `return_code=-200` while preserving rank logs.

+

 - Added separate compile-time debug flag `BEAM_DEBUG_STREAM4_HISTOGRAM_TRACE`. When `BEAM_ENABLE_DEBUG=ON`, the dispatcher logs `stream4_histogram_trace` before Stream4 graph launch and after Stream4 slot completion: rank, physical/logical shard, slot, active/inactive histogram index, clean/dirty counts, current threshold slot/value, A/B totals, active/inactive less/equal counts, deltas versus `clean_count`, scratch count, queue state, and Stream3/Stream4 job counters. Kaggle diagnostic notebooks enable the flag. No Stream4 algorithm behavior changed.
 - Verification after `BEAM_DEBUG_STREAM4_HISTOGRAM_TRACE`: `git diff --check` passed, both Kaggle notebooks parsed as JSON, Docker debug `production_runner` build passed with `BEAM_DEBUG_STREAM4_HISTOGRAM_TRACE=ON`, and targeted CUDA tests passed 3/3 (`threshold_cuda_tests`, `dispatcher_cuda_tests`, `static_memory_cuda_tests`).
+- Added Kaggle diagnostic runner auto-stop config `RUN_TIMEOUT_SEC=200`. The timeout applies only to spawned `production_runner` rank processes after setup/build, terminates live ranks after 200 seconds, kills remaining ranks after a 10-second grace period, and returns `return_code=-200` while preserving rank logs.
*** End Patch

+
+Requested run:
